Method: 采用多尺度、无需训练、基于显著性的自底向上视觉注意模型，直接处理低分辨率事件输入，并对事件流施加最多256倍下采样因子，从中选取感兴趣区域（ROI）。

Result: 在Prophesee Automotive数据集上实现鲁棒的多尺度ROI选择，可检测多种车辆类型、行人、交通灯和交通标志等多类目标，准确率最高达70.8%，时间分辨率为毫秒级，比数据集真值时间分辨率精细16倍。

Conclusion: 早期事件下采样与基于显著性的注意机制相结合，可作为高效边缘神经形态视觉系统的有效前端方案。

Abstract: Neuromorphic vision systems operate under strict constraints on bandwidth, memory, and energy, particularly at the edge, motivating early mechanisms for data reduction and selective processing. In this work, we investigate a multi-scale training-free, saliency-based, bottom-up visual attention model that operates directly on low-resolution event-based input and selects Regions of Interest (ROI) from the visual scene. The model is evaluated across multiple downscaling factors applied to the incoming event stream, with input resolutions reduced by up to 256x relative to full resolution. Performance is assessed on the Prophesee Automotive dataset, the largest publicly available event-based dataset, demonstrating robust ROI selection across different scales on a real-world use-case. The proposed approach is capable of detecting ROIs belonging to multiple object classes, including various vehicle types, pedestrians, traffic lights, and traffic signs, with accuracy up to 70.8%, while operating at millisecond temporal resolution, 16x finer than the temporal resolution provided by the dataset ground truth. These results highlight the potential of combining early event downscaling with saliency-based attention as an effective front-end for efficient edge neuromorphic vision systems.

</details>


### [74] [From Foundation Embeddings to Cropland Maps: Label Efficiency, Temporal Transferability and Independent Human Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17138)
*Mohammad Ammar Mughees,Giovanni Montefoschi,Zhongxin Chen,Maria Antonia Brovelli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在缅因州，使用AlphaEarth年度地理空间嵌入配合轻量分类器进行耕地/非耕地二分类，无需微调即达93.7%总体精度；盲评中优于CDL参考产品，与微调TerraMind模型差异不显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地理空间基础模型可提供卫星影像的可复用表征，减少下游制图所需的任务特定建模；本研究旨在评估年度AlphaEarth嵌入能否在有限标注条件下支持区域尺度的耕地识别。

Method: 使用192个空间分离的影像块，标签来自USDA CDL；对冻结的基础模型嵌入（不微调）训练逻辑回归、梯度提升集成与最近类质心分类器；开展跨年份（2018–2023）同区域迁移实验；在2023年一个连片区块随机抽取385点，与双解释员盲评共识比较，并与CDL及微调TerraMind分割模型对比。

Result: 留出块上总体精度93.7%、平衡精度90.8%；逻辑回归与梯度提升集成相差0.3个百分点以内，最近类质心（无参数拟合）达90.2%；6万平衡像素样本与860万全量像素相差1.3个百分点以内；跨年份迁移保持精度；盲评中AlphaEarth+随机森林与共识一致率95.3%（κ=0.82），CDL为91.7%（κ=0.72，McNemar p=0.0161），与微调TerraMind差异不显著（95.3% vs 93.5%，p=0.14）。

Conclusion: 冻结的地理空间嵌入可作为区域耕地制图的低计算量候选方案；结果提示可能部分平滑了CDL标签噪声，但未证实可全州范围修正参考产品。研究受限于单州案例、30 m派生训练参考及单一区块人工验证。

Abstract: Geospatial foundation models provide reusable representations of satellite imagery that support downstream mapping with limited task-specific modelling. We evaluate whether annual AlphaEarth embeddings support binary cultivated-versus-non-cultivated mapping in Maine, USA, using 192 spatially separated patches and labels derived from the USDA Cropland Data Layer (CDL). Without fine-tuning the foundation model, a lightweight classifier reaches 93.7% overall accuracy and 90.8% balanced accuracy on held-out patches. Logistic regression is within 0.3 percentage points of a gradient-boosted ensemble, while a nearest-class-centroid rule, which uses class centroids but fits no parameters, reaches 90.2%. A balanced sample of 60,000 labelled pixels is within 1.3 percentage points of the full pool of 8.6 million pixels; because pixels are spatially autocorrelated, this result concerns pixel-sample efficiency rather than 60,000 independent annotation sites. In a same-region transfer experiment, classifiers trained in one year remain accurate across 2018 to 2023. Against a blind, two-interpreter consensus at 385 randomly sampled points in one contiguous 2023 block, the AlphaEarth-plus-random-forest map agrees at 95.3% ($κ=0.82$), compared with 91.7% for the CDL ($κ=0.72$; exact two-sided McNemar $p=0.0161$). This local result is consistent with partial smoothing of CDL label noise, but it does not establish statewide correction of the reference product. On the same points, the difference from a fine-tuned TerraMind segmentation model is not statistically significant (95.3% versus 93.5%; $p=0.14$), and the experiment is not a controlled comparison of computational cost. These results support frozen geospatial embeddings as a low-compute candidate for regional cropland mapping, subject to the limits of a single-state study, a 30 m-derived training reference, and a one-block human validation.

</details>


### [75] [ResLRP: The Role of Residual Cancellation in Attribution Instability in Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17152)
*Jim Berend,Reduan Achtibat,Daniel Schäffer,Alexander Binder,Wojciech Samek,Sebastian Lapuschkin,Maximilian Dreyer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ResLRP方法，通过显式处理ViT残差连接中的抵消效应，解决归因爆炸问题，显著提升归因质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ViT中常用的LRP归因方法常产生噪声大、不忠实的解释。作者发现关键在于残差连接的处理：残差路径中的抵消效应导致归因爆炸，且这种抵消在ViT中比语言Transformer更强。

Method: 提出Residual-aware Layer-wise Relevance Propagation (ResLRP)，是LRP的简单扩展，其传播规则显式考虑残差分支中的抵消，具有精确保守性，并可证明地约束归因爆炸。通过因果通道干预验证残差抵消是驱动不稳定性的原因。

Result: ResLRP在忠实性和定位性上显著提升归因质量，覆盖监督、自监督、对比、层级及多模态ViT架构及FunnyBirds基准。在现代VLM中增益最大，定位提升27-29%，忠实性最高提升3.4倍。此外可定位SAE特征，残差放大度量可作为架构级诊断。

Conclusion: 残差抵消是ViT归因不稳定的核心原因，ResLRP通过显式建模抵消有效改善归因，并为架构级归因质量诊断提供工具。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) are central to most modern vision models, yet obtaining input attributions that are fine-grained, faithful, and stable remains challenging. Layer-wise Relevance Propagation (LRP) has been adapted to transformer attention, but in ViTs it often produces noisy, unfaithful explanations. We show that the missing ingredient is the treatment of residual connections: cancellation effects in residual pathways lead to attribution explosion. Moreover, we find that these cancellations are substantially stronger in ViTs than in language transformers. To address this issue, we introduce Residual-aware Layer-wise Relevance Propagation (ResLRP), a simple extension of LRP whose propagation rules explicitly account for cancellations in residual branches, are exactly conservative, and provably bound relevance explosion. Causal channel-wise interventions confirm that residual cancellation, not a generic regularization effect, drives the instability. ResLRP substantially improves attribution quality across faithfulness and localization, evaluated on ViT architectures spanning supervised, self-supervised, contrastive, hierarchical, and multimodal families, as well as on the ground-truth-controlled FunnyBirds benchmark. The largest gains arise in modern Vision Language Models (VLMs), with +27-29% localization and up to 3.4x faithfulness scores. Beyond benchmarks, ResLRP localizes Sparse Autoencoder (SAE) features in input space, and our residual amplification measure serves as an architecture-level diagnostic predicting where attribution degrades.

</details>


### [76] [MUMINS: Metadata-conditioned Uncertainty-aware Medical Image Next-state Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17169)
*Anna Oliveras,Roger Marí,Rafael Redondo,Oriol Guardià,Cynthia Ifeyinwa Ugwu,Ana Tost,Bhalaji Nagarajan,Carolina Migliorelli,Vicent Ribas,Petia Radeva*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出MUMINS，一种高效的扩散框架，能够单次反向扩散过程中联合生成随访医学影像并预测空间不确定性图，通过元数据条件化与基线软锚定保留细粒度解剖结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测肿瘤生长、神经退行等解剖变化是极具挑战的生成任务。形态演化相对静态解剖而言变化细微、高度患者特异且本质随机。现有方法存在问题：确定性网络忽略生物随机性，而标准扩散模型需要计算代价高昂的多轮采样才能量化不确定性。

Method: MUMINS联合扩散基线扫描与其随访残差，两者相加合成随访影像，同时在单次反向扩散过程中预测空间不确定性图。方法以时间间隔和相关元数据为条件，在每一步去噪时动态将基线作为软锚点重新注入以保留细粒度解剖结构，并用负对数似然头学习不确定性图以显式标记易出错区域。该架构不含器官特异性启发式，通过针对不同数据集的独立重训练即可复用于多种解剖部位。

Result: 广泛评估表明，针对特定数据集重训练的MUMINS在肺CT（PNG）和脑MRI（OASIS-3）上匹配或超越了各自领域专用的最新方法。

Conclusion: MUMINS是一种无需器官特异性设计、可跨解剖结构复用、且能高效量化不确定性的医学图像下一状态合成框架，性能达到或优于领域专用最新方法。项目页面：https://github.com/aolivtous/MUMINS。

Abstract: Forecasting anatomical changes such as tumor growth and neurodegeneration is a challenging generative vision task. Morphological evolution is subtle relative to static anatomy, highly patient-specific, and inherently stochastic. Existing methods struggle with several issues: deterministic networks ignore biological stochasticity, while standard diffusion models require computationally prohibitive multi-pass sampling to quantify uncertainty. We propose MUMINS (Metadata-conditioned Uncertainty-aware Medical Image Next-state Synthesis), an efficient diffusion framework that jointly diffuses a baseline scan and its follow-up residual, summed to synthesize the follow-up scan, while concurrently predicting a spatial uncertainty map, in a single reverse diffusion process. Conditioned on the time interval and relevant metadata, it preserves fine-grained anatomy by dynamically re-injecting the baseline as a soft anchor at every denoising step, and a negative-log-likelihood head learns the uncertainty map to explicitly flag error-prone regions. Designed without organ-specific heuristics, the same architecture is reused across anatomies via separate, dataset-specific retraining. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that dataset-specific retraining of MUMINS matches or outperforms dedicated, domain-specific state-of-the-art methods on lung CT (PNG) and brain MRI (OASIS-3). Project page: https://github.com/aolivtous/MUMINS.

</details>


### [77] [Multimodal Cultural Heritage Architectural Style Classification for Residential Buildings in the UAE Based on CLIP Embeddings and SVM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17181)
*Ahmed Ammar Kubba,Manar Abu Talib,Iman Ibrahim,Qassim Nasir*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多模态机器学习框架，基于OpenAI的CLIP模型融合图像视觉特征与专家文本描述，对阿联酋住宅建筑风格进行分析与分类，在八个风格聚类上取得98%的分类准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文化遗产建筑风格的分析与分类面临两大挑战：一是建筑图像视觉信息复杂，传统CNN分类方法高度依赖图像而忽视文本描述；二是针对非西方地区的专用数据集相对匮乏。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用OpenAI的CLIP模型构建多模态框架，将图像视觉特征与专家文本描述特征融合为统一的512维嵌入；随后使用UMAP进行降维以可视化，并采用K-Means进行无监督聚类；聚类标签由人工分析K-Means结果得出，用于训练SVM分类器以实现建筑风格的自动分类。

Result: 在识别出的八个风格聚类上，分类准确率达到98%，高于文献中所有其他研究，证明了结合视觉与文本模态的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文展示了多模态AI在支持建筑遗产分析方面的潜力，为探索地区性建筑身份提供了可扩展且可解释的工具。

Abstract: The analysis and classification of cultural heritage architectural styles remain challenging due to the complexity of visual images of buildings, which are highly relied on in traditional CNN-based classification approaches in comparison to textual descriptions, and the relative lack of non-western region-specific datasets. This paper addresses this gap by proposing a multimodal machine learning framework to analyze and classify Emirati residential architecture using OpenAI's CLIP model. We integrate visual features from images and textual features from expert descriptions into a unified 512-dimensional embedding, followed by dimensionality reduction with UMAP for visualization and unsupervised clustering using K-Means. Cluster labels, which are derived from manual analysis of the K-Means clusters, are used to train an SVM classifier for automated architectural style classification. Our approach achieves a classification accuracy of 98% across eight identified style clusters, higher than every other study in the literature, demonstrating the effectiveness of combining visual and textual modalities. Overall, this paper highlights the potential of using multimodal AI to support architectural heritage analysis, offering scalable and interpretable tools for exploring regional architectural identities.

</details>


### [78] [EventEgoHands++: Event-based Egocentric 3D Hand Mesh Reconstruction with Real Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17189)
*Ryosei Hara,Wataru Ikeda,Masashi Hatano,Mariko Isogawa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 EventEgoHands++ 框架，用于第一视角事件相机 3D 手部网格重建，通过手部检测器和自适应注意力解决左右手区分与交互建模问题，并构建大规模真实数据集 EEH-R。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统相机在低光和运动模糊下表现不佳，事件相机凭借高动态范围和高时间分辨率被引入该任务。但第一视角下相机佩戴者的运动产生密集背景事件，掩盖手部信号；已有的第一视角事件方法虽用手部分割缓解该问题，却仅用二值掩码，无法区分左右手，导致缺乏实例级信息、在只有单手或无手时仍预测双手，从而产生错误的双手关系和较低的重建精度。

Method: 方法包含两部分：一是 Hand Detector，估计左右手各自的实例级边界框与掩码；二是 Adaptive Attention，依据检测结果动态地对注意力进行门控，以准确学习双手之间的空间关系和相互交互。此外扩展合成数据集 N-HOT3D，并新建 EEH-R 真实第一视角事件手部数据集。

Result: EEH-R 是目前规模最大的真实事件第一视角手部数据集，包含约 100 万帧带标注数据，覆盖低光等环境。在合成和真实数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法持续优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过实例级手部检测与自适应注意力机制，EventEgoHands++ 有效缓解了第一视角事件手部重建中的背景干扰和左右手混淆问题，并在合成与真实数据上取得一致提升，为事件相机下的第一视角手部重建提供了新框架与大规模数据支持。

Abstract: 3D hand mesh reconstruction is a challenging yet essential task for downstream applications, including human-robot interaction and AR/VR. Although conventional cameras have been widely adopted for this task, methods that rely on them struggle in low-light environments and under severe motion blur. To address these limitations, event-based cameras have recently attracted attention for their high dynamic range and high temporal resolution. However, applying event cameras to egocentric hand reconstruction remains challenging because camera wearer's motion produces dense background events that obscure hand-specific signals. Although the first egocentric event-based approach mitigates this issue using hand segmentation, its binary hand mask does not distinguish between left and right hands. As a result, the model lacks instance-level hand information and predicts both hands even when only one or neither hand is present. This limitation leads to incorrect inter-hand relationships and degraded reconstruction accuracy. In this paper, we propose EventEgoHands++, a framework for event-based 3D hand mesh reconstruction from an egocentric viewpoint. The proposed method incorporates a Hand Detector that estimates instance-level bounding boxes and masks for both the left and right hands. Moreover, we introduce Adaptive Attention, which dynamically gates the attention based on these detection results to accurately learn the spatial relationship and mutual interactions between the hands. To train and evaluate our framework, we extend the synthetic N-HOT3D dataset and newly construct EEH-R, the largest real-world event-based egocentric hand dataset to date, comprising approximately 1M annotated frames captured in environments including low-light conditions. Extensive experiments on both synthetic and real datasets demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms the baselines.

</details>


### [79] [Probe-VAD: Ordinal Likelihood Probing for Training-Free Video Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17211)
*Jiawei Gu,Qilin Zhao,Tengkuo Guo,Zhiming Zhong,Shuangqing Zhang,Fan Lyu,Fang Zhao,Guo-Sen Xie,Caifeng Shan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Probe-VAD 是一种免训练的序数二分类探测框架，通过直接询问冻结的视觉语言模型（VLM）的严重程度偏好，将视觉理解转化为连续、对排序敏感的异常分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于VLM的免训练视频异常检测（VAD）在"视觉理解"与"异常打分"之间存在受限的接口：基于字幕的流程将视觉证据压缩成文本，可能丢失细微线索；直接数值生成则迫使模型只能用少量预设分数表达判断。这些接口会模糊异常严重程度的细微差异，使视觉上不同的片段获得相似的表示或分数，限制了异常排序的分辨率。

Method: 提出 Probe-VAD，一种序数二分类探测（ordinal binary-probing）框架，直接从冻结的VLM中探测严重程度偏好。给定原始视频片段，查询十个有序的严重程度阈值，提取受约束的 YES/NO 续写似然；归一化后的偏好构成累积严重程度轮廓（cumulative severity profile）；从中聚合尾部证据得到连续异常分数，并用等渗投影（isotonic projection）强制序数一致性。

Result: 在公开VAD基准上取得更优性能，且计算成本低。无需任务特定训练，也无需基于字幕的压缩。

Conclusion: Probe-VAD 提供了一种简单接口，可将冻结VLM的视觉理解转化为连续、对排序敏感的异常分数，克服了字幕压缩与预设分数接口的分辨率限制。

Abstract: Video anomaly detection (VAD) aims to localize anomalous events in untrimmed videos. Vision-language models (VLMs) provide rich visual understanding for training-free VAD, but existing approaches impose restrictive interfaces between visual understanding and anomaly scoring. Caption-based pipelines compress visual evidence into text, potentially discarding subtle cues, while direct numerical generation forces the model to express its judgment through a small set of predefined scores. Such interfaces can obscure subtle differences in anomaly severity, causing visually distinct clips to receive similar representations or scores and thereby limiting the resolution of anomaly ranking. We propose \textbf{Probe-VAD}, an ordinal binary-probing framework that directly probes severity preferences from a frozen VLM. Given raw video clips, Probe-VAD queries ten ordered severity thresholds and extracts constrained \textit{YES}/\textit{NO} continuation likelihoods. Their normalized preferences form a cumulative severity profile, from which tail evidence is aggregated into a continuous anomaly score, with isotonic projection enforcing ordinal consistency. Experiments on public VAD benchmarks demonstrate superior performance with low computational cost. Probe-VAD provides a simple interface for translating frozen VLM visual understanding into continuous, rank-sensitive anomaly scores without task-specific training or caption-based compression. Code is available at: https://github.com/yvestine/COVAS-VAD.

</details>


### [80] [InfoTaxa: Information-Calibrated Label-Free Clustering for Fine-Grained Visual Taxonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17218)
*David Ahmedt-Aristizabal,Mohammad Ali Armin,Lars Petersson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在BIOSCAN-5M上，无标签聚类在科/属级表现良好，但在种级遇到瓶颈；作者提出InfoTaxa，把聚类效率与DNA审计信号分离，证明种级瓶颈既受聚类方法限制，也受图像表示信息量限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无标签、免标注的细粒度生物多样性监测具有可扩展价值，但仅依赖图像的聚类存在"由粗到细"的失效模式：能恢复较粗的分类结构，却在种级停滞；需要诊断这一瓶颈究竟来源于聚类方法不足，还是图像表示本身信息不足。

Method: 在BIOSCAN-5M上做信息校准的聚类分析：用BioCLIP 2特征配合UMAP与HDBSCAN，并与oracle-K、图方法和学习式聚类头对比；提出InfoTaxa，将聚类效率（探针估计的图像信息中被无监督划分恢复的比例）与配对DNA审计信号结合，DNA仅用于审计而非推理输入；并用留出的晚期融合探针评估加入DNA后种级预测误差的降低程度。

Result: BioCLIP 2+UMAP+HDBSCAN达到科级0.79 AMI、属级0.67 AMI，优于先前图像基线，并可与oracle-K、图方法和学习式聚类头竞争。密度聚类流程在目级和科级分别恢复了约0.90和0.81的图像可用信息；晚期融合探针显示加入DNA使种级预测误差降低约2比特。鲁棒性分析覆盖多种图像编码器、已描述物种与稀有类子集、探针诊断、留出物种的粗级别泛化与同物种检索。

Conclusion: 在所测试设定下，种级无标签聚类同时受"聚类能力限制"和"表示能力限制"：改进聚类可恢复更多图像已暴露的结构，但仅靠图像无法弥合经DNA审计所揭示的信息鸿沟。

Abstract: Label-free clustering of frozen pretrained visual embeddings offers a scalable route to biodiversity monitoring, but image-only fine-grained taxonomy exhibits a consistent coarse-to-fine failure mode: clusters recover broad taxonomic structure yet plateau at species level. We study this behaviour on BIOSCAN-5M through an information-calibrated clustering analysis. BioCLIP~2 features with UMAP and HDBSCAN reach $0.79$ AMI at family and $0.67$ at genus, substantially improving over the prior image baseline and remaining competitive with oracle-$K$, graph-based, and learned clustering heads on the same frozen features. To diagnose whether the remaining plateau is method-limited or information-limited, we introduce InfoTaxa, which combines clustering efficiency---the fraction of probe-estimated image information recovered by an unsupervised partition---with paired DNA as an audit signal only, not an inference input. The density pipeline recovers approximately $0.90$ and $0.81$ of the image-available information at order and family, respectively. Held-out late-fusion probes show that adding DNA to the image embedding reduces species-level prediction error by approximately two bits. Robustness analyses cover multiple image encoders, described-species and rare-class subsets, probe diagnostics, and held-out-species coarse-rank generalisation and same-species retrieval. Thus, in the tested setting, species-level label-free clustering is both clustering-limited and representation-limited: improved clustering may recover additional image-exposed structure, but cannot close the DNA-audited information gap alone.

</details>


### [81] [FROD: Feature Matching Residual Denoising Oracle Bone Decipher](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17227)
*Yanbin Hou,Biao Xiong,Guojun Xu,Jianwen Xiang,Cheng Tan,Yanchao Yang,Junwei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文将甲骨文释读辅助任务形式化为跨时代图像翻译，提出 FROD 方法，通过快速特征匹配门控分割监督、残差去噪扩散模型与多阶段字体风格化精修，提升甲骨文到现代汉字的生成与识别效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 甲骨文释读依赖专家人工分析，过程劳动密集。甲骨文与现代汉字虽差异大，但在部首层面常保留局部拓扑不变量，因此可借助跨时代图像翻译方法辅助释读，缓解专家负担。

Method: FROD 包含三个核心设计：1) 训练时利用快速特征匹配提供门控分割监督，高匹配度样本按 patch 对齐细粒度部首，低相似度样本整体训练以避免错配伪影；2) 采用残差去噪扩散模型（RDDM）联合估计噪声与残差信号，减少标准扩散模型中的位置漂移和笔画紊乱；3) 使用多阶段字体风格化精修网络消除边缘噪声、稳定笔画结构。

Result: 在增强的字符不相交数据集上，FROD 的 Top-1 识别准确率高于所评估的基线方法，相较 OBSD 取得 3.8% 的绝对提升。

Conclusion: 将甲骨文释读辅助建模为跨时代图像翻译是可行且有效的，FROD 通过特征匹配门控监督、残差去噪扩散与字体风格化精修，在字符不相交设定下优于现有基线，为甲骨文释读提供自动化辅助手段。

Abstract: Oracle bone script (OBS), one of the earliest Chinese writing systems, plays an important role in the study of Chinese etymology. Traditional decipherment relies heavily on domain experts who analyze characters through semantic context and structural evolution. To assist this labor-intensive process, we formulate OBS decipherment assistance as a cross-era image translation task and propose FROD (Feature Matching Residual Denoising Oracle Bone Decipher). Although many OBS characters differ substantially from their modern counterparts, they often preserve local topological invariants at the radical level. During training, FROD leverages fast feature matching to provide gated segmentation supervision: paired samples with sufficient matches are processed patch-wise to align fine-grained radicals, whereas low-similarity pairs are trained holistically to avoid mismatched artifacts. In addition, a Residual Denoising Diffusion Model (RDDM) jointly estimates noise and residual signals, thereby reducing the positional drift and stroke disorder commonly observed in standard diffusion models. Finally, a multi-stage font stylization refinement network refines the generated images by eliminating edge noise and stabilizing stroke structures. On our augmented character-disjoint dataset, FROD achieves higher Top-1 recognition accuracy than the evaluated baselines, with a 3.8% absolute gain over OBSD.

</details>


### [82] [DecoGS: Adaptive Static-Dynamic Decoupling of 3D Gaussians for Free-Viewpoint Video Streaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17230)
*Idil Sulo,Alexey Supikov,Ilke Demir,Sainan Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DecoGS提出一种选择性优化的3D高斯泼溅在线训练方法，仅针对动态或光度变化区域更新，实现高效、无闪烁的流式3D重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式3D重建方法每帧更新所有高斯，即使在静态区域也做冗余更新，导致闪烁和漂移，同时牺牲速度。论文旨在兼顾速度与时间一致性，减少静态区域的冗余优化。

Method: DecoGS引入自适应机制，将优化聚焦于存在运动或光度变化的时空区域，实现有针对性的训练。同时结合区域感知的高斯管理，通过梯度门控与高效可见性滤波，维持时间连贯性和紧凑内存占用，无需大规模预训练。

Result: 在N3DV和MeetRoom数据集上分别达到34.55 dB和31.60 dB PSNR，超越所有流式和离线基线；渲染速度261 FPS，时间闪烁较最优先前方法降低70倍，且无需大规模预训练。

Conclusion: DecoGS通过选择性、区域感知的优化策略，有效解决了流式3D高斯重建中静态区域冗余更新引发的闪烁与漂移问题，在精度、速度和时间稳定性上均取得优越表现。

Abstract: Streaming 3D reconstruction demands both speed and temporal fidelity, goals that existing methods undermine by updating every Gaussian every frame, even in static regions. We present DecoGS, a method for efficient online training of 3D Gaussians from streaming videos. Unlike prior methods that update the entire scene indiscriminately, DecoGS introduces an adaptive mechanism that selectively focuses optimization on spatiotemporal regions exhibiting motion or photometric changes. This targeted training strategy eliminates redundant updates that cause flickering and drift in nominally static regions, while enabling fast, high-fidelity scene updates. The pipeline further integrates region-aware Gaussian management through gradient gating and efficient visibility filtering to maintain temporal coherence and a compact memory footprint. On N3DV and MeetRoom, DecoGS achieves 34.55 and 31.60 dB PSNR respectively, outperforming all streaming and offline baselines, while rendering at 261 FPS with $70\times$ lower temporal flicker than the best prior method, requiring no large-scale pretraining.

</details>


### [83] [Video-HolmesV2: Can MLLMs Reason with Spatio-Temporal Audio-Visual Evidence in Long Videos?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17248)
*Zhaoyang Wei,Zipeng Wang,Yushe Cao,Chenhui Qiang,Shuaibing Cheng,Xuesong Yang,Sen Nie,Bowen Jiang,Wenchao Ding,Yanchao Hao,Zheng Wei,Xuehui Yu,Zhenjun Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Video-HolmesV2 基准与音视频引导的 Token 压缩框架，面向深度音视频耦合的长叙事视频推理，采用基于证据的评估方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大模型视频理解评测过度依赖视觉线索、忽视听觉信息，导致模型成为"沉默观察者"，绕过真正的跨模态推理；同时密集采样带来证据与上下文的权衡：增加帧数以捕获证据会引起注意力分散和 token 爆炸。

Method: 提出 Video-HolmesV2 基准，强调基于证据的评估，要求模型用精确的时空音视频证据支撑答案；构建多模型交叉验证流程保证任务严谨性；提出时空证据感知指标进行细粒度校准；并提出音视频-文本引导的 Token 压缩框架，融合任务意图与听觉锚点蒸馏高价值推理线索以缓解长上下文噪声。

Result: 评估中，即使强专有模型准确率也低于 60%，而所提方法优于同类开源全模态模型。

Conclusion: 该工作通过证据驱动的音视频耦合基准与音视频引导的 Token 压缩方法，推动长视频叙事推理向真正的跨模态理解发展。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models have demonstrated impressive video understanding, yet their ability to reason over long-form narratives is often masked by visual-centric evaluations and inefficient context processing. Existing benchmarks over-rely on visual heuristics while marginalizing auditory cues, effectively reducing models to "silent observers" that bypass genuine cross-modal reasoning. Moreover, standard dense sampling creates an evidence-context trade-off: increasing frames to capture evidence inevitably leads to attention distraction and token explosion. To bridge these gaps, we present Video-HolmesV2, a novel benchmark designed for Deep Audio-Visual Coupling. Unlike previous works, it enforces an Evidence-Based Evaluation, requiring models to justify answers with precise spatio-temporal audio-visual evidence, thereby reducing confounding effects of guessing and hallucinated evidence. To support this, we introduce: (1) a Multi-Model Cross-Verification pipeline to ensure task rigor; (2) a Spatio-temporal Evidence-Aware Metric for fine-grained calibration. Furthermore, we propose an Audio-Text Guided Token Compression framework. By fusing task intent with auditory anchors, our method distills high-value reasoning cues to mitigate long-context noise. In our evaluation, even strong proprietary models achieve below 60% accuracy, while our approach outperforms comparable open-source omni-models.

</details>


### [84] [Exploring 2D backbone effects for indoor semantic occupancy prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17257)
*Shizhang Fanga,Wanling Yea,Qi Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在EmbodiedScan的RGB-D占用预测管线中，仅替换2D图像骨干即可显著改变占用精度；DINOv2与BLIP2明显优于CLIP系列，且超过原始ResNet-50基线，说明图像骨干是3D语义占用预测中的主要变数来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在RGB-D语义占用预测管线（如EmbodiedScan）中，图像编码器常被当作默认模块而未被审慎设计，但其特征正是后续采样到3D体素网格的视觉证据。作者希望直接研究这一设计选择对最终3D预测的影响。

Method: 固定RGB-D投影、深度分支和占用头，仅替换2D图像骨干，在受控设置下对比CLIP-ResNet、CLIP-ViT、BLIP2和DINOv2四种编码器，并考察类别级表现。

Result: mIoU差异显著：DINOv2为30.55%，BLIP2为29.49%，CLIP-ViT为24.33%，CLIP-ResNet为17.41%。更强的编码器在不改动下游3D融合管线的情况下超过EmbodiedScan的ResNet-50基线。DINOv2在布局与结构类别上更强，BLIP2在部分以物体为中心的类别上接近；CLIP-ViT明显优于CLIP-ResNet，表明CLIP特征以稠密token形式暴露的方式对体素提升很重要。

Conclusion: 图像骨干并非具身语义占用预测中的次要工程细节，而是最终3D预测差异的主要来源之一；更换2D骨干带来的精度提升超过若干精心设计的占用架构或模块。

Abstract: Semantic occupancy prediction gives an embodied agent a voxel-level account of where space is free, occupied, and semantically meaningful. In RGB-D pipelines such as EmbodiedScan, the image encoder is often left as a default module, even though its features are the visual evidence later sampled into the 3D grid. We study this design choice directly. A central finding is that changing the 2D backbone improves occupancy accuracy more than several carefully designed occupancy architectures or modules. We keep the main RGB-D projection, depth branch, and occupancy head fixed, and replace only the image backbone. The compared encoders are CLIP-ResNet, CLIP-ViT, BLIP2, and DINOv2. Under the controlled setting, the measured mIoU changes substantially: DINOv2 obtains 30.55\%, BLIP2 obtains 29.49\%, CLIP-ViT obtains 24.33\%, and CLIP-ResNet obtains 17.41\%. The stronger encoders also exceed the original EmbodiedScan ResNet-50 baseline without modifying the downstream 3D fusion pipeline. Class-level results give a more detailed picture: DINOv2 is stronger on many layout and structural categories, whereas BLIP2 remains close on several object-centered classes. CLIP-ViT improves clearly over CLIP-ResNet, showing that the way CLIP features are exposed as dense tokens matters for voxel lifting. These results indicate that the image backbone is not a secondary engineering detail in embodied semantic occupancy, but a major source of variation in the final 3D prediction.

</details>


### [85] [Semantic-Spatial Agreement Verification for Mitigating Object Hallucination in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17269)
*Ziheng Ren,Qian Gao,Jun Fan,Guohui Ding,Zhenyu Yang,Yuteng Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出免训练的语义-空间一致性验证方法 SSAV，通过多提示语义聚合与区域定位一致性检测物体幻觉，在多模型与评测协议上有效降低幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型在药物辅助、无障碍感知与环境决策等场景中可能生成图中不存在的物体，这类幻觉会带来现实安全风险，需要可解释的外部验证手段。

Method: SSAV 为免训练方法：聚合多个语义等价提示以估计语义支持并降低措辞敏感性；Query-Induced Regional Verification (QIRV) 结合跨查询区域持续性、空间重叠与候选相对优势，识别孤立高响应与分散定位；用几何平均融合语义与空间证据，任一支路支持不足则降低验证分数。

Result: 在三个基座模型与多种评测协议上有效缓解物体幻觉：LLaVA-1.5-7B 在 COCO、A-OKVQA、GQA 上平均准确率在 POPE Popular 与 Adversarial 下分别提升 1.81 与 3.17 个百分点，CHAIRs 从 49.40% 降至 32.80%。

Conclusion: 跨查询语义稳定性与区域一致性可为物体陈述提供可解释的外部视觉证据，从而有效抑制物体幻觉。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models generate natural-language responses from visual inputs, yet may mention objects absent from an image. In medication assistance, accessible perception, and environmental decision-making, such hallucinations can create real-world safety risks. We propose Semantic-Spatial Agreement Verification (SSAV), a training-free method for verifying object claims. A visually grounded claim should remain stable across semantically equivalent queries and repeatedly localize to the same image region. SSAV aggregates multiple prompts to estimate semantic support and reduce sensitivity to query wording. Query-Induced Regional Verification (QIRV) combines cross-query region persistence, spatial overlap, and relative candidate dominance to identify isolated high responses and dispersed localizations. A geometric mean fuses semantic and spatial evidence, lowering the verification score when either branch lacks support. Experiments on three base models and multiple evaluation protocols show that SSAV effectively mitigates object hallucination. On LLaVA-1.5-7B, accuracy averaged across COCO, A-OKVQA, and GQA improves by 1.81 and 3.17 percentage points under POPE Popular and Adversarial, respectively, while CHAIRs decreases from 49.40% to 32.80%. These results show that cross-query semantic stability and regional consistency provide interpretable external visual evidence for object claims.

</details>


### [86] [Optical-Flow Wingbeat Counting in MuJoCo: A Comparison of Convolutional, Spiking, and Attention-Based Temporal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17308)
*Zhang Nengbo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文在MuJoCo中对扑翼飞行器的"振翅计数"进行受控评估：利用Crazyflie虚拟相机所观测的有符号光流，将共用空间卷积编码器分别与因果时序卷积网络、循环LIF脉冲网络、因果自注意力结合，预测相位与活动量，再用同一穿越事件计数器计数；在1.5 m与3.0 m光距下，三类模型精确计数准确率分别为96.67%/95.00%/96.67%与94.44%/92.22%/95.00%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对扑翼飞行器进行视觉监测时，需要从运动强度与平均频率中区分出单次振翅，而现有工作缺乏对光流式振翅计数的受控评估，也未说明不同时序建模架构是否可比较，以及是否需要同时报告总计数与事件级指标。

Method: 构建240组配对场景、共1,440段虚拟相机光流片段，按场景3:1划分训练测试；采用统一的空间卷积编码器，分别接因果时序卷积、循环leaky integrate-and-fire脉冲网络与因果自注意力三种时序模型；各模型输出相位与活动量，再由相同的定向穿越事件计数器完成计数；测试前冻结全部模型参数（含6个既有卷积模型与12个新模型）。

Result: 1.5 m处三类模型精确计数准确率为96.67%、95.00%、96.67%；3.0 m处为94.44%、92.22%、95.00%。所有配对场景自助法区间均包含零，说明差异不显著。另有7段远距离脉冲模型片段总数正确但事件时序不匹配。

Conclusion: 结果支持在所测试设定下进行因果光流振翅计数的可行性，并揭示了边界敏感型误差；但无法据此对架构进行排序，也未验证重复训练、真实飞行鲁棒性与硬件效率，且总计数与事件级测量必须同时报告。

Abstract: Visual monitoring of flapping-wing vehicles requires distinguishing individual wingbeats from motion strength and average frequency. This paper presents a controlled MuJoCo evaluation of wingbeat counting from signed optical flow observed by virtual cameras mounted on Crazyflie vehicles. Three flapping-wing models were recorded at optical distances of 1.5 and 3.0 m, producing 1,440 clips from 240 paired scene configurations with a scene-level 3:1 training-test split. A common spatial convolutional encoder was combined with a causal temporal convolutional network, a recurrent leaky integrate-and-fire spiking network, or causal self-attention. Each model predicted phase and activity, followed by the same directed-crossing event counter. The six existing convolutional models were retained, and all twelve new models were frozen before their test predictions were generated. Exact-count accuracies at 1.5 m were 96.67%, 95.00%, and 96.67%, respectively; at 3.0 m they were 94.44%, 92.22%, and 95.00%. All paired scene-bootstrap intervals for differences in exact-count accuracy included zero. Seven far-distance spiking-model clips had correct totals despite event-timing mismatches, demonstrating why total-count and event-level measurements must be reported together. The results support the feasibility of causal optical-flow counting in the tested setting and identify boundary-sensitive errors. They do not establish an architecture ranking across repeated training, real-flight robustness, or hardware efficiency.

</details>


### [87] [PanoGS-SLAM: Panoramic 3D Gaussian Splatting SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17387)
*Yongqi Mao,Hao Shi,Yufan Zhang,Zhonghua Yi,Xiangfei Guo,Kaiwei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PanoGS-SLAM，首个基于3D高斯泼溅的全景稠密SLAM系统，在球面域直接进行可微渲染与位姿优化，实现全向光度约束，提升跟踪与建图稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS-based SLAM多面向窄视场针孔相机，角覆盖有限削弱位姿可观测性，在快速运动和大幅视角变化下光度优化不稳定。全景相机具备全向覆盖优势，有望缓解此问题。

Method: 在球面域进行可微渲染与位姿优化，提供全向光度约束；引入球面一致性光度损失以补偿等距柱状投影的面积畸变；提出深度引导的高斯初始化策略，稳定新观测区域的增量建图。

Result: 在真实与合成全景基准（PALVIO、SynPano）上，跟踪精度和渲染质量均优于几何与GS-based基线，前端收敛快且达到实时性能。受控视场实验显示随角覆盖增大，优化条件与收敛稳定性单调改善。

Conclusion: PanoGS-SLAM验证了 sensing geometry 对可微高斯SLAM优化景观的根本影响，全景感知几何显著提升优化条件与稳定性。

Abstract: Real-time dense SLAM is a core capability for robotics applications that require robust localization and high- quality mapping in dynamic or fast-changing environments. Recent 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS)-based SLAM methods have shown promising performance, but most are designed for narrow-FoV pinhole cameras, where limited angular coverage weakens pose observability and often leads to unstable photo- metric optimization under rapid motion and large viewpoint changes. We present PanoGS-SLAM, the first panoramic dense SLAM system built on 3D Gaussian Splatting. Our method per- forms differentiable rendering and pose optimization directly in the spherical domain, enabling omnidirectional photometric constraints for more stable tracking. To improve geometric consistency and robustness, we introduce (1) a sphere-consistent photometric loss that compensates for the area distortion of equirectangular projection, and (2) a depth-guided Gaussian initialization strategy that stabilizes incremental mapping in newly observed regions. Extensive experiments on both real and synthetic panoramic benchmarks (PALVIO and SynPano) show that PanoGS-SLAM consistently outperforms geometric and GS-based baselines in tracking accuracy and rendering quality, while achieving fast front-end convergence and real-time perfor- mance. In addition, controlled field-of-view experiments reveal a clear monotonic improvement in optimization conditioning and convergence stability as angular coverage increases, high- lighting the fundamental role of sensing geometry in shaping the optimization landscape of differentiable Gaussian-based SLAM. The source code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [88] [SSC-Priors: Exploring Semantic and Visibility Priors to Boost Lidar Semantic Scene Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17413)
*Tetiana Martyniuk,Jonathan Seele,Alexandre Boulch,Gilles Puy,Renaud Marlet,Raoul de Charette*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出用语义伪标签和可见性信息两种简单先验作为额外输入，仅需极小改动即可提升现有激光雷达语义场景补全（SSC）网络的性能，在SemanticKITTI和SSCBench-nuScenes上均有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年SSC方法多追求架构创新，使模型越来越重、越来越复杂（如联合训练点云语义分割分支）。作者希望退一步，探索无需复杂架构重设计的简单策略来提升现有网络性能。

Method: 将两类先验直接作为额外输入提供给给定SSC网络，仅需对原架构做最小适配：(1) 语义伪标签——由现成分割器为输入点云赋予语义伪标签；(2) 可见性信息——区分激光雷达与扫描点之间的空白空间和视线外的未知空间，并研究表示可见性信息的数据设计空间。

Result: 语义伪标签显著提升现有SSC模型性能；借助oracle评估证明高质量语义先验是语义增益（mIoU）的主要驱动，SSC模型可用真值语义只训练一次，之后无需重训即可配合任意分割器使用。可见性信息在测试架构上带来次级性能提升，并用自由空间标签的oracle界定了剩余提升空间。在SemanticKITTI上，四种架构下旧模型可与SOTA竞争，其中一种甚至超越SOTA；在SSCBench-nuScenes上，两种先验在更稀疏的32线传感器下也能迁移。

Conclusion: 简单先验（语义伪标签与可见性信息）作为额外输入即可有效提升现有SSC网络，无需复杂架构重设计，为SSC性能提升提供了低成本、易迁移的方案。

Abstract: This paper investigates easy strategies to boost the performance of existing networks for lidar semantic scene completion (SSC) without requiring complex architectural redesigns. The fact is that, over the last years, SSC methods have mostly pursued architectural innovations, making the models heavier and more complex, e.g., by jointly training a point cloud semantic segmentation branch. In this work, we take a step back and explore two priors used as simple ingredients (possibly noisy) to improve existing approaches: semantic pseudo-labels and sensor visibility information. Concretely, we provide both kinds of information directly as additional inputs to a given SSC network, requiring only a minimal adaptation of the original architecture. We first demonstrate that endowing input point clouds with semantic pseudo-labels from off-the-shelf segmenters significantly improves the performance of existing SSC models. In fact, by evaluating these models against an oracle, we establish that high-quality semantic priors are a primary driver of semantic gains (mIoU), and that the SSC model can be trained just once with ground-truth semantics and then exploited without retraining using any segmenter. Furthermore, we equip the input lidar point cloud with visibility information that distinguishes between empty spaces (between the lidar and a scanned point) and unknown spaces (outside of lines of sight), providing a secondary performance boost across the tested architectures. We study the design space of data for representing visibility information and bound the remaining headroom with a ground-truth oracle on the free-space labels. On SemanticKITTI, these enhancements make older models competitive with state-of-the-art systems across four architectures, in one case even outperforming them. On the SSCBench-nuScenes benchmark, both priors also transfer with the sparser 32-beam sensor.

</details>


### [89] [Tracking the Unseen: An Occlusion-Robust Framework for Target Tracking Under Full and Long-Term Occlusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17427)
*Mais Mohammed,Sharifa Mohammed,Hanan Awadh,Haneen Bamaas,Raghad Bawazeer,Elham Alghamdi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种抗遮挡多目标跟踪框架，融合YOLOv11n检测、卡尔曼滤波预测与遮挡感知外观重识别，在OVIS和自建数据集上均优于OccluTrack。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时多目标跟踪系统在完全遮挡和长期遮挡下极为脆弱，目标暂时或完全离开视野时，传统跟踪器会过早终止轨迹，导致身份丢失，削弱监控等应用中的态势感知能力。

Method: 框架分为目标检测、遮挡期间位置估计、目标重现后身份恢复三阶段，集成YOLOv11n检测器、卡尔曼滤波运动预测和遮挡感知的外观重识别。在相同条件下评估了六种Re-ID架构，最终选用表现最佳的遮挡感知掩码网络（OAMN）。

Result: 在公开OVIS数据集上对比OccluTrack，MOTA相对提升18.1%，IDF1相对提升25.1%，身份切换减少12.8%。在模拟长期遮挡的自建监控数据集上，MOTA达0.734、IDF1达0.729，相对OccluTrack分别提升14.2%和5.8%。

Conclusion: 该框架在挑战性遮挡条件下展现出强跟踪连续性、稳健身份保持和可靠轨迹估计能力，适合需要持续目标跟踪的监控类应用。

Abstract: Real-time multi-object tracking systems remain highly vulnerable to full and long-term occlusion, where targets temporarily or completely disappear from the camera's field of view. Conventional trackers may terminate trajectories prematurely, resulting in identity loss and reduced situational awareness in applications such as defense and surveillance. This work proposes an occlusion-robust target tracking framework that maintains target identity and trajectory continuity through the integration of YOLOv11n object detection, Kalman Filter motion prediction, and occlusion-aware appearance-based re-identification. The framework consists of three stages: object detection, position estimation during occlusion, and identity recovery after target reappearance. Six Re-Identification (Re-ID) architectures were evaluated within the same tracking framework under identical conditions, with the Occlusion-Aware Mask Network (OAMN) achieving the best overall performance and therefore selected for the final pipeline. The framework was benchmarked against OccluTrack on the public OVIS dataset, achieving relative improvements of 18.1 percent in Multiple Object Tracking Accuracy (MOTA) and 25.1 percent in Identity F1 Score (IDF1), while reducing identity switches by 12.8 percent. On a custom military dataset simulating surveillance and battlefield-like environments with long-term occlusion, the framework achieved a MOTA of 0.734 and an IDF1 of 0.729, corresponding to relative improvements of 14.2 percent and 5.8 percent over OccluTrack. The system demonstrated strong tracking continuity, robust identity preservation, and reliable trajectory estimation under challenging occlusion conditions, highlighting its effectiveness for defense-related surveillance applications requiring continuous target tracking during visibility loss.

</details>


### [90] [BrainFocus: EEG-Guided ROI Selection for Efficient Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17443)
*Yihui Peng,Guorui Lu,Qinyu Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 BrainFocus，一种以 EEG 信号引导的可靠高效 VLM 框架：EEG 分类器预测目标类别，YOLO 定位对应 ROI，仅当两者均通过置信度阈值时将裁剪 ROI 输入 VLM，否则处理整图。在 40 类基准上，该框架在杂乱场景提升 VQA 准确率 4.14–9.87 pp，同时减少输入 token、总 token 与端到端 FLOPs 约 23.2%–39.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM 在 VQA 上表现优异，但处理大尺寸杂乱图像时计算开销高，而实际只有小区域相关。EEG 可提供人类对视觉刺激的神经响应，作为 ROI 的语义线索，然而 EEG 引导的视觉类别解码尚不完美，直接用于 ROI 路由不可靠。

Method: BrainFocus 采用双通道决策：EEG 分类器预测目标类别，YOLO 检测器定位匹配 ROI；仅当两个预测都通过置信度阈值时，VLM 只接收裁剪后的 ROI，否则处理完整图像。评估方面，基于 EEG-ImageNet 构建 40 类基准，包含生成的杂乱图像与真实以物体为中心的图像，并配有目标 ROI 标注和 600 个英文视觉问答对。

Result: 在 Qwen3.5-VL 2B、4B 和 9B 模型上，BrainFocus 在杂乱场景中将 VQA 准确率提升 4.14–9.87 个百分点，同时输入 token 与总 token 分别减少 23.2%–39.4% 和 23.2%–39.3%，端到端 FLOPs 减少 23.2%–39.5%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，即使 EEG 的语义解码并不完美，EEG 仍可有效引导 VLM 实现高效推理，在提升准确率的同时显著降低计算开销。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) achieve strong visual question answering (VQA) performance, but processing large cluttered images is computationally expensive when only a small region is relevant. Electroencephalography (EEG) signals, which capture human neural responses to visual stimuli, can provide a human-derived semantic cue about the region of interest (ROI). However, EEG-guided visual category decoding remains imperfect, making direct ROI routing unreliable. In this work, we propose BrainFocus, a reliable EEG-guided efficient VLM framework for VQA. An EEG classifier predicts a target category, and a YOLO detector localizes the matching ROI. The VLM receives the cropped ROI only when both predictions pass confidence thresholds; otherwise, it processes the full image. For evaluation, we build on EEG-ImageNet to construct a 40-class benchmark comprising generated cluttered images and real object-centric images, with target-ROI annotations and 600 English visual question-answer pairs. Across Qwen3.5-VL 2B, 4B, and 9B models, BrainFocus improves VQA accuracy by 4.14-9.87 percentage points (pp) on cluttered scenes while reducing input tokens and total tokens by 23.2%-39.4% and 23.2%-39.3%, and end-to-end floating-point operations (FLOPs) by 23.2%-39.5%. These results demonstrate that EEG can guide efficient VLM inference even when its semantic decoding is imperfect.

</details>


### [91] [ORCA: Occlusion-Aware Refinement and Completion for Novel View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17450)
*Weronika Jakubowska,Maciej Zięba,Przemysław Spurek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ORCA是一种遮挡感知的单图三维场景重建与补全方法，通过区分小遮挡与大遮挡分别采用RGB-D修复和生成式补全，减少对生成模型的过度依赖，在DIV2K上优于VistaDream。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单图新视角合成本质上是病态问题。相机远离输入视角时，原本被遮挡的区域显现，暴露缺失几何与空洞。现有方法常依赖生成模型补全这些区域，但许多伪影其实只是深度边界附近的小缝隙，并不需要生成新的场景内容，生成式补全反而会引入幻觉。

Method: 提出ORCA，一种遮挡感知的可探索三维场景重建与补全方法。先用单目深度将三维结构引入高斯锚点（Gaussian-anchor）表示，同时保持原相机射线对应关系。探索场景时按尺寸和结构处理缺失区域：小遮挡用重建中已有的RGB-D信息修复，生成式修补仅保留给无法从场景可靠恢复的大区域。新增高斯锚点在局部优化，不修改已有表示。

Result: 在DIV2K上，ORCA在所有报告指标上均优于VistaDream，MUSIQ从61.60提升到68.71，CLIP-IQA从0.474提升到0.574。

Conclusion: 结果表明，许多新视角伪影可通过复用重建场景中已有信息有效修复，减少不必要的生成式修补能限制生成引起的幻觉，更好地保留原场景的内容与结构。

Abstract: Novel-view synthesis from a single image is a fundamentally ambiguous problem. As the camera moves away from the input viewpoint, previously hidden regions become visible, exposing missing geometry and holes in the reconstructed scene. Existing methods often rely on generative models to complete such regions. However, many of these artifacts are small gaps near depth boundaries and do not require generating new scene content.
  In order to eliminate expensive process of generating image we introduce ORCA, an occlusion-aware method for reconstructing and completing explorable 3D scenes from a single image. ORCA first introduces 3D structure into a Gaussian-anchor representation using monocular depth while preserving the original camera-ray correspondence. During scene exploration, missing regions are handled based on their size and structure. Small disocclusions are repaired using RGB-D information already available in the reconstruction, while generative inpainting is reserved for larger regions that cannot be reliably recovered from the scene. New Gaussian anchors are added and optimized locally without modifying the existing representation. By reducing unnecessary reliance on generative inpainting, ORCA limits generation-induced hallucinations and better preserves the content and structure of the original scene.
  On DIV2K, ORCA improves novel-view quality over VistaDream across all reported metrics, increasing MUSIQ from 61.60 to 68.71 and CLIP-IQA from 0.474 to 0.574. These results show that many novel-view artifacts can be repaired effectively by reusing information already present in the reconstructed scene.

</details>


### [92] [Tables Decoded: DELTA for Structure, TARQA for Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17458)
*Jahanvi Rajput,Dhruv Kudale,Saikiran Kasturi,Utkarsh Verma,Ganesh Ramakrishnan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出基于结构化文本表示的表格理解方法，包含DELTA（表格结构识别）和TARQA（基于LLM的表格问答），在多语言表格任务上取得与SOTA相当或更优的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格理解方法主要依赖视觉语言模型（VLM）处理表格图像，但这种方式可扩展性差、需要语言特定的视觉编码器，对多语言文档不友好。论文希望用更易处理、更贴合LLM的结构化文本表示来替代图像输入。

Method: 将物理结构识别、逻辑结构识别与OCR分离，准确提取布局和内容；用紧凑统一的OTSL（优化表格结构语言）格式输出表格，编码单元格排布与文本内容。在此基础上提出TARQA——在OTSL序列上微调的LLM，用于表格问答。

Result: 在TSR任务上，DELTA在FinTabNet、PubTabNet、PubTables-1M上的TEDS-Structure分数与SOTA相当；在自建的印地语基准TORQUE上验证了非英语表格鲁棒性；TARQA在WTQ上提升9.3个百分点，在FinTabNetQA上提升9.2个百分点；在TORQUE上排名第二（仅次于所有VLM与DELTA+LLM变体中的第一名）。

Conclusion: 基于结构化文本表示（OTSL）的路线可有效替代图像输入，在表格结构识别与表格问答任务上兼顾准确性与多语言适用性，并已开源代码、模型和基准。

Abstract: Table understanding is a core task in document intelligence, encompassing two key subtasks: table reconstruction and table visual question answering (TabVQA). While recent approaches predominantly rely on vision- language models (VLMs) operating on table images, we propose a more scalable and effective alternative based on structured textual representations. These representations are easier to process, align more naturally with LLMs, and eliminate the need for language-specific visual encoders, making them particularly suitable for multilingual documents. We present DELTA, which separates physical structure recognition, logical structure recognition, and OCR to extract both layout and content accurately. DELTA outputs tables in Optimised Table Structure Language (OTSL), a compact and unified format that encodes cell arrangements and textual content. On table structure recognition (TSR), DELTA achieves TEDS- Structure scores comparable with state-of-the-art methods across FinTabNet, PubTabNet, and PubTables-1M. We further establish its robustness on non-English tables through our curated Hindi benchmark, TORQUE. Building on this, we introduce TARQA, an LLM fine-tuned on OTSL sequences. Our approach yields gains of 9.3 p.p. on WTQ (TabQA) and 9.2 p.p. on FinTabNetQA (TabVQA), respectively. On TORQUE, our method ranks second among all VLMs and DELTA + LLM variants. We release our code, models, and benchmark at: https://github.com/Tihiitborg/Tables-Decoded

</details>


### [93] [Det-LIME: Detector-Aware, Multi-Instance Local Interpretable Model-Agnostic Explanations for Automated Marine Mammal Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17479)
*Jiayi Zhou,David W. Johnston,Brinnae Bent*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Det-LIME——面向检测器的多实例解释方法，可为每个检测框生成独立、对齐的高分辨率解释，在港海豹无人机影像上优于现有 LIME 变体与梯度归因方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑箱目标检测器在海洋哺乳动物研究中应用迅速，但可解释性技术很少融入保护工作流程。现有分类导向的可解释工具不适合检测任务：面对群居或集群生活的生物影像时，忽略场景中的多个检测，仅输出单实例结果，导致个体间证据混淆；且生成的解释分辨率低、常缺乏生物学意义，难以用于调试、针对性数据增强和精细数据采集。

Method: 提出 Det-LIME，即 LIME 的检测器感知、多实例适配版本。核心设计包括：每次检测的独立加权、强调各检测框附近区域的邻近核，以及基于交并比（IoU）的匹配机制，以在扰动过程中追踪同一实例。在港海豹检测的无人机航拍影像上进行评估，并增加海鸟案例研究以检验通用性，与 vanilla LIME、Stabilized LIME、Deterministic LIME 及梯度归因方法对比。

Result: 使用归因比（Attribution Ratio）和最大显著性命中率（Max Saliency Hit Rate）两项指标，Det-LIME 在多实例归因上持续优于对比方法，且解释分辨率更高、具备实例感知能力。

Conclusion: Det-LIME 能为模型输出提供更契合检测任务、更具生物学意义的解释，支持后处理与调试，并可指导建模、数据采集或数据增强的实际改进，有助于将可解释性纳入保护工作流程。

Abstract: Despite the rapid uptake of black-box object detectors in marine mammal research and monitoring, explainability techniques are rarely integrated into conservation workflows. Furthermore, most classification-oriented explainability tools are ill-suited to detection tasks involving imagery of social organisms or those with colonial life histories, as they ignore multiple detections within a scene and produce single-instance outputs that blur evidence across individuals. These methods also generate low-resolution, often biologically irrelevant visuals, limiting their utility for debugging, targeted data augmentation, and refined data collection.
  We proposed Det-LIME, a detector-aware, multi-instance adaptation of Local Interpretable Model-Agnostic Explanations (LIME) that produced instance-specific, box-aligned explanations by combining per-detection weighting, a proximity kernel that emphasizes regions near each box, and Intersection-over-Union-based matching to track the same instance across perturbations. We evaluated Det-LIME on aerial drone imagery for harbor seal detection, with an additional seabird case study to assess generality, and compared it with vanilla LIME, Stabilized LIME, Deterministic LIME, and gradient-based attribution methods.
  Using the Attribution Ratio and Max Saliency Hit Rate metrics, we showed that Det-LIME consistently improved multi-instance attribution. In practice, these higher-resolution, instance-aware explanations provide insight into model outputs and support post-processing, debugging, and actionable improvements in modeling and data collection or augmentation.

</details>


### [94] [PhysStream: Streaming Physics-Grounded Video Generation with Structured Scene Memory and Fine-Grained Motion Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17521)
*Chuhao Chen,Peter Wonka,Chaoyang Wang,Chen Wang,Qiao Feng,Sergey Tulyakov,Lingjie Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PhysStream，一个自回归的图像到视频生成模型，通过结构化场景记忆与稀疏速度增量信号实现物理落地的细粒度交互式运动控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可控视频生成方法存在两大局限：要么需要在生成开始前提供完整控制序列，无法在生成过程中交互调整；要么使用像素空间信号，仅规定物体位置而非物理动力学，难以表达真实的物理动态。因此需要一种支持生成中控制、并以物理量为条件的物理落地视频生成方法。

Method: PhysStream是一个自回归图像到视频生成模型，引入结构化场景记忆（由已生成帧在线推导的位置图和物体跟踪图），并支持通过稀疏速度增量信号进行细粒度运动控制，让模型学习底层动力学。训练分两阶段：先以运动控制条件微调双向模型，再训练带结构化场景记忆的因果自回归模型以进一步提升物理一致性。

Result: 在多物体桌面刚体场景上实现生成中交互控制（此前方法不支持），在合成基准上将运动分布距离（FVMD）降低33%、轨迹误差降低12%；在真实场景对比中超过85%的情况下被人类评估者偏好。

Conclusion: PhysStream通过结构化场景记忆与物理量化的速度增量控制，实现了支持生成过程中交互控制的物理落地图像到视频合成，在数值指标与人类偏好上均优于现有基线。

Abstract: Interactive control for video generation is moving from coarse prompts toward fine-grained, physically meaningful manipulation of dynamic scenes. Yet existing controllable methods either require the full control schedule before generation starts, or use pixel-space signals that dictate object positions rather than physical dynamics. To address these limitations, we propose PhysStream, an autoregressive model for physics-grounded image-to-video synthesis that incorporates structured scene memory---positional maps and object tracking maps derived online from previously generated frames---and supports fine-grained motion control via sparse velocity-increment signals that encode physical quantities, letting the model learn the underlying dynamics. We train our model in two stages: a bidirectional model is first finetuned with motion-control conditioning, then a causal autoregressive model is trained with additional structured scene memory, further improving physical consistency. PhysStream enables interactive, mid-generation control over multi-object tabletop rigid-body scenes---a capability not supported by prior methods---reducing motion distribution distance (FVMD) by 33% and trajectory error by 12% over the strongest baselines on synthetic benchmarks, and is preferred by human evaluators in over 85% of in-the-wild comparisons. Please check our website for more details: https://czzzzh.github.io/PhysStream

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [95] [Bi-MoDe: Bilateral Control-based Imitation Learning via Modifier-Conditioned Decoding for Modulation of Execution Speed and Contact Intensity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16040)
*Takumi Kobayashi,Masato Kobayashi,Yuki Uranishi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Bi-MoDe，一种修饰条件解码框架，将受约束的隐变量通过adaLN-Zero注入Transformer动作解码器每层，实现基于双边控制的模仿学习中行为指令（快慢、轻重力）的可控执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于双边控制的模仿学习同时包含位置与力信息，适合接触丰富操作，但现有方法难以让操作者在推理时指定任务执行方式（如快慢、轻柔或用力）。

Method: 提出Bi-MoDe修饰条件解码框架，通过adaLN-Zero将受约束隐变量注入Transformer动作解码器每一层，使行为指令直接影响动作块（action-chunk）生成；在真实白板擦拭任务上评估时间与物理修饰的组合效果。

Result: Bi-MoDe在物理指令遵循方面优于动作块基线，同时保持相当的时间控制能力。消融实验显示解码器条件化与隐空间组合存在相互作用，二者结合对精确遵循物理指令很重要。

Conclusion: 通过将修饰隐变量注入解码器各层，可在保持时间控制的同时提升物理行为指令的遵循能力，且解码器条件化与隐空间组合的协同是关键。

Abstract: Bilateral control-based imitation learning captures both position and force information, making it well suited to contact-rich manipulation. However, existing approaches provide limited means for an operator to specify how a learned task should be executed at inference time, such as slowly or quickly, gently or firmly. We propose Bi-MoDe, a modifier-conditioned decoding framework that injects a constrained latent into every layer of the Transformer action decoder via adaLN-Zero, allowing behavioral directives to directly influence action-chunk generation. We evaluate the method on a real-world whiteboard wiping task with combinations of temporal and physical modifiers. Bi-MoDe improves physical directive following over the action-chunking baseline while maintaining comparable temporal control. An ablation further shows that decoder conditioning and latent-space composition interact, and that their combination is important for accurate physical directive following. Additional material is available at the https://mertcookimg.github.io/bi-mode/

</details>


### [96] [MR-GLi: Mixed Reality-Based Gripper-Linked Overlays for Underwater Robot Arm Teleoperation via Bilateral Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16041)
*Masashi Sasago,Masato Kobayashi,Yuki Uranishi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MR-GLi混合现实界面，将反作用力矩指示器与腕部相机图像空间配准到机器人夹爪上，与2D显示器对比用于水下双边遥操作，任务表现相当但主观负担降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉力矩反馈可支持水下双边遥操作，但混合现实相较传统显示器呈现方式是否具有优势尚不明确。

Method: 20名参与者在刚性与柔性物体上完成举升和抓取放置任务，采用被试内平衡设计对比MR-GLi与2D显示器，两者视觉反馈内容相同并均使用四通道双边控制。

Result: MR-GLi提供与夹爪联动的视觉反馈获取方式，力矩调节表现与2D显示器相近；主观评价显示在作业空间与视觉反馈之间切换注意力的感知负担降低。

Conclusion: 验证了夹爪联动式MR叠加层用于水下双边遥操作的可行性，并强调除任务表现外还需考虑信息获取方式。

Abstract: Visual torque feedback supports underwater bilateral teleoperation, but the benefit of mixed reality (MR) over conventional monitor presentation remains unclear. We present MR-GLi, an MR interface that spatially registers a reaction torque indicator and wrist-camera image to the robot gripper. Twenty participants performed lift and pick-and-place tasks with rigid and compliant objects in a counterbalanced within-subject comparison with a 2D monitor, using identical visual-feedback content and four-channel bilateral control. MR-GLi provided gripper-linked access to visual feedback while maintaining a similar level of torque-regulation performance to the 2D monitor. Subjective evaluation further indicated reduced perceived burden associated with shifting attention between the workspace and visual feedback. These results demonstrate the feasibility of gripper-linked MR overlays for underwater bilateral teleoperation and highlight the importance of considering information access in addition to task performance. Additional material: https://mertcookimg.github.io/mr-gli/

</details>


### [97] [World-Action Models for Robot Learning and Control: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16074)
*Zuxing Lu,Hongjia Zhai,Guanzhi Wang,Huajian Zeng,Jiaqi Yang,Jingyu Liu,Lei Cheng,Yuantai Zhang,Yuheng Qiu,Zezhou Cheng,Ivan Laptev,Danfei Xu,Benjamin Riviere,Giuseppe Loianno,Eric Xing,Xingxing Zuo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文是一篇面向机器人学的"世界-动作模型"(WAMs)综述,系统厘清了WAMs的概念边界,提出统一分类法,并总结应用、评测资源与关键挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放环境中的机器人受部分可观测、物理约束和动态任务上下文影响,仅将观测与语言映射为动作不足够,还需预判候选动作对未来的影响。世界模型、视频生成与VLA策略的进展催生了将未来世界预测与可执行动作生成耦合的WAMs,但缺乏面向机器人学的系统性梳理。

Method: 综述方法:厘清WAMs与传统世界模型、基于模型的强化学习、动作条件视频生成及反应式VLA策略的范围差异;提出统一分类法,覆盖表征、转移建模、动作接口、架构、训练流程、数据模态与扩展策略;梳理操作、导航、自动驾驶应用;归纳数据集、基准、指标与评测协议。

Result: 形成WAMs的统一分类体系与应用/评测资源汇总,并识别出动作对齐、世界-动作解耦、空间与多视角一致性、长时程记忆、用于闭环策略学习的神经仿真以及高效推理等关键挑战。

Conclusion: 该综述为将预测性世界建模与动作生成相结合提供了简洁的技术基础,指向更可靠的具身机器人智能。

Abstract: Robots operating in open environments act under partial observability, physical constraints, and dynamic task contexts. Beyond mapping observations and language instructions to actions, they must anticipate how candidate actions may affect future states and task-relevant outcomes. Recent advances in world models, video generation, and Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies have motivated the development of World-Action Models (WAMs), which couple future world prediction with executable action generation. This survey provides a robotics-oriented review of WAMs. We clarify their scope relative to conventional world models, model-based reinforcement learning, action-conditioned video generation, and reactive VLA policies, and organize existing methods through a unified taxonomy covering representations, transition modeling, action interfaces, architectures, training pipelines, data modalities, and scaling strategies. We further review applications of WAMs in manipulation, navigation, and autonomous driving, and we summarize the datasets, benchmarks, metrics, and protocols used to evaluate WAM systems. Finally, we discuss key challenges in action alignment, world-action factorization, spatial and multi-view consistency, long-horizon memory, neural simulation for closed-loop policy learning, and efficient inference. Taken together, this survey aims to provide a concise technical foundation for integrating predictive world modeling with action generation, toward more reliable embodied robot intelligence. Project page: https://rcl-robotics.github.io/Awesome-World-Action-Models.

</details>


### [98] [AssemblyGrid v1: A Benchmark for Multi-Robot Production with Temporary Coalitions, Local Information, and Geometric Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16075)
*Fouad Bahrpeyma,David Heik,Dirk Reichelt*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 AssemblyGrid v1，一个可复现的多机器人柔性生产基准，统一建模工序推进、物料转运、临时协作与并发执行，并支持集中式与去中心化（含 MARL）方法对比评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 柔性机器人生产需要工序推进、物料路由、资源分配、临时协作与并发执行的联合决策，各决策相互影响可行性；在去中心化控制下，机器人仅有局部信息，而系统进展依赖集体决策、共享资源、物料状态与工作空间兼容性，这给决策带来更大挑战。这些特性与部分可观测、资源竞争下的合作式多智能体决策高度契合，但缺乏统一、可复现的基准。

Method: 提出 AssemblyGrid v1 基准，在单一任务级公式中集成显式工序推进、去中心化观测、物料转移、临时多机器人联盟、生产性并发以及依赖几何的可行性；设计 Flow、Coalition、Concurrency 三类工作负载族，每类含三个场景层级。任务成功与评估指标独立于学习奖励与求解方法定义，使学习类与非学习类方法可求解同一生产问题。评估包括可执行一致性检查、机制研究，以及使用特权集中式参考、结构化去中心化控制器与 IPPO、MAPPO、QMIX 等 MARL 方法的算法实验。

Result: 实验表明在集中式与去中心化控制下均可实现生产性执行；MARL 实验进一步显示去中心化策略能够从局部观测与动作中学习到有效的生产行为。

Conclusion: AssemblyGrid 可作为研究柔性机器人生产中合作式决策的可控基准，支持集中式与去中心化方法的统一比较。

Abstract: Flexible robotic production requires joint decisions on process progression, material routing, resource assignment, temporary cooperation, and simultaneous execution, since each decision can affect the feasibility of the others. The challenge is greater under decentralized control, where each robot acts from bounded local information while system progress depends on collective decisions, shared resources, material state, and workspace compatibility. These properties closely match cooperative multi-agent decision making under partial observability and resource contention. This paper introduces AssemblyGrid v1, a reproducible benchmark for repeated multi-robot production that combines explicit process progression, decentralized observations, material transfer, temporary multi-robot coalitions, productive concurrency, and geometry-dependent feasibility within one task-level formulation. The benchmark includes Flow, Coalition, and Concurrency workload families, each with three scenario levels. Task success and evaluation measures are defined independently of learning reward and solution method, allowing learning-based and non-learning methods to address the same production problem. AssemblyGrid v1 is evaluated through executable conformance checks, mechanism studies, and algorithmic experiments using a privileged centralized reference, structured decentralized controllers, and MARL methods including IPPO, MAPPO, and QMIX. Results demonstrate productive execution under centralized and decentralized control. The MARL experiments further show that decentralized policies can learn effective production behavior from local observations and actions, supporting AssemblyGrid as a controlled benchmark for studying cooperative decision making in flexible robotic production.

</details>


### [99] [Structure-Preserving Quantum Circuit Architectures for Robot Kinematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16089)
*Andrea Morghen,Pierluigi Arpenti,Roberto Schiattarella,Giovanni Acampora,Bruno Siciliano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向刚体变换的量子表示与电路架构，并应用于串联开链机械臂的DH运动学，用单量子比特Bloch向量编码方向、选择器寄存器加权求和，实现可由Pauli观测值读出并可重构末端位姿的紧凑几何接口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化空间数据需要既能保持几何关系、又可暴露可测量观测量、还能在有限深度硬件上实现的量子编码。现有工作缺乏对刚体变换（尤其机械臂DH运动学）兼具几何可解释性与硬件可行性的量子表示方案。

Method: 将每个平移项分解为经典度量幅值与由单个量子比特Bloch向量编码的带符号单位方向；用参数化旋转复现坐标架方向的有序传播；引入选择器寄存器制备与幅值成比例的概率分布，使指定读出量子比特的约化态编码归一化加权和；再结合保留的经典尺度重构度量意义下的末端位置，并另用两个读出量子比特编码末端坐标架轴向。

Result: 在理想期望值层面，测得的Pauli观测值可复现相应经典运动学量；串联机械臂验证中位置与姿态重构误差在理想模拟下数值可忽略。有限采样模拟、含噪执行、转译分析与硬件演示进一步刻画了统计误差、噪声敏感性与实现开销。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了刚体变换及串联开链机械臂DH运动学的几何可解释量子表示与多种电路实现方案，在量子比特数、电路深度、受控操作与测量需求间提供不同权衡，并完成从理想仿真到含噪执行与硬件验证的系统性评估，但未主张计算优势。

Abstract: Structured spatial data require quantum encodings that preserve geometric relations, expose measurable observables, and remain implementable on finite-depth hardware. This work introduces a quantum representation and circuit architecture for rigid-body transformations and specializes it to Denavit--Hartenberg kinematics of serial open-chain manipulators. Each translational contribution is factorized into a classical metric magnitude and a signed unit direction encoded by a single-qubit Bloch vector, while parameterized rotations reproduce the ordered propagation of frame directions. A selector register prepares probabilities proportional to the contribution magnitudes, and the reduced state of a designated readout qubit encodes their normalized weighted sum. The retained classical scale then reconstructs the metric end-effector position. Two additional readout qubits encode terminal-frame axes, providing a compact and geometrically interpretable pose interface. At the ideal expectation-value level, measured Pauli observables reproduce the corresponding classical kinematic quantities. Alternative circuit architectures realize the same representation with different tradeoffs in qubit count, circuit depth, controlled operations, and measurement requirements. Validation on a serial manipulator yields numerically negligible position and orientation reconstruction errors under ideal simulation. Finite-shot simulations, noisy executions, transpilation analysis, and a hardware demonstration further characterize statistical error, noise sensitivity, and implementation overhead without asserting computational advantage.

</details>


### [100] [Occupancy Network-Guided Autonomous Robotic Partial Nephrectomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16186)
*Ethan Kilmer,Pit Henrich,Jiawei Ge,Paul M. Scheikl,Laura Connolly,Soum D. Lokeshwar,Joseph Chen,Justin D. Opfermann,Kaitlyn Kumar,Lauren Shepard,Ahmed Ghazi,Nirmish Singla,Richard J. Cha,Kevin Cleary,Franziska Mathis-Ullrich,Axel Krieger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 首个视觉引导的自主软组织肿瘤切除系统，实现全瘤切除（部分肾切除），在体模上完成8次连续自主切除，全部切缘阴性，平均绝对切缘误差1.61±0.48 mm。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主软组织肿瘤手术长期局限于器官表面干预，因为现有系统在组织变形或被切开后无法感知并适应解剖结构。要实现完整的肿瘤切除，系统必须能在单视角、部分观测且组织不断变形切割的条件下，持续推断完整三维解剖并自适应调整。

Method: 提出首个视觉引导的自主系统：核心是条件占用网络（conditional occupancy networks），完全在基于物理的仿真中训练，可从单视角部分点云推断完整3D解剖（肿瘤、切缘组织、肾脏），并在组织被切割变形时维持术中跟踪，支持自适应规划与执行。平台集成深度相机（采集表面点云）、双臂机器人（电外科切割与真空组织操控）及自主切除控制策略。在患者来源的水凝胶体模、开放部分肾切除条件下验证。

Result: 在患者来源水凝胶体模上完成8次连续自主肿瘤切除，共77次电外科切割，所有切割均达到阴性手术切缘，平均绝对切缘误差为1.61±0.48 mm。

Conclusion: 该工作首次为监督式自主、闭环、影像驱动、切缘阴性的肿瘤切除提供了基础（在体模中验证），突破了自主软组织手术仅限于器官表面的限制。

Abstract: Autonomous soft-tissue cancer surgery has been limited to interventions on organ surfaces, because current systems cannot perceive and adapt to anatomy once it deforms or is cut. We introduce the first vision-guided autonomous system capable of performing complete tumor resections for partial nephrectomy. Our system integrates conditional occupancy networks, trained entirely in a physics-based simulation, that infer full 3-D anatomy (tumor, margin tissue, and kidney) from single-view partial point clouds. These occupancy networks maintain intraoperative tracking even as tissue is cut and deformed, enabling adaptive planning and execution. The surgical platform combines a depth camera for capturing surface point clouds, dual robotic arms for electrosurgical cutting and vacuum-based tissue manipulation, and an autonomous control strategy for tumor resection. In patient-derived hydrogel phantoms under an open partial nephrectomy setting, the robot performed eight consecutive autonomous tumor resections comprising 77 electrosurgical cuts, with all cuts achieving negative surgical margins and 1.61 $\pm$ 0.48 mm mean absolute margin error. This work demonstrates, for the first time, a foundation for supervised autonomous closed-loop, imaging-driven, margin-negative tumor removal in phantoms.

</details>


### [101] [Tendon-Driven Continuum Robot with Modular Stiffness and In-Situ Self Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16256)
*Guo Ning,Sue,Zheng Cao,Junzhe Hu,Xiangyun Bu,David Quinn,Tiancheng Wu,Zackory Erickson,Carmel Majidi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种自包含模块化连续体机器人平台，结合机械可重构性与本体位姿估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有连续体机器人多为任务专用，且依赖外部传感基础设施，限制了适应性和实际部署。

Method: 采用可互换的连续体关节（刚度经解析预计算）实现快速组装与形状编程；利用磁传感器与模块化学习框架进行本体感知，每个关节训练一个模型并跨配置复用。

Result: 在真实环境中实验验证了系统的自感知能力与适应性，无需外部跟踪。

Conclusion: 该平台实现了无需外部传感的可重构连续体机器人自感知与自适应。

Abstract: Continuum robots enable smooth shape morphing and safe interaction in confined environments. However, most existing systems are task-specific and depend on external sensing infrastructure, limiting their adaptability and real-world deployment. This paper presents a self-contained modular continuum robotic platform that combines mechanical reconfigurability with onboard pose estimation. The robot is constructed from interchangeable continuum joints with analytically precomputed stiffness, allowing rapid assembly and direct programming of the robot shape. Proprioceptive sensing is achieved using magnetic sensors and a modular learning-based framework, where a single model is trained per joint and reused across configurations. The system is experimentally validated in real world, demonstrating self-sensing capabilities and adaptation without external tracking.

</details>


### [102] [ConGraspXL: Controllable Constraint-Conditioned Dexterous Grasping Motion Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16319)
*Hui Zhang,Mirko Meboldt,Jie Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ConGraspXL 在 GraspXL 基础上扩展出可控约束条件下的抓取运动合成，支持多种任务驱动约束及其组合，提供即插即用的底层抓取控制器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧抓取通常面向特定任务，带来异构约束（特定接近方向、期望接触区域、指定手腕轨迹、功能手姿等）。此前工作 GraspXL 能实现可扩展的抓取运动合成，但缺乏在此类任务驱动约束下合成的可控性。

Method: 提出分层约束表示，通过掩码残差接口实现灵活约束组合，并利用动态手部中心与前馈手腕引导提升控制精度。

Result: 在保持 GraspXL 强泛化能力的同时，实现对各单个约束及其组合的精确灵活控制。

Conclusion: ConGraspXL 可作为即插即用底层抓取控制器，服务于全身抓取补全、功能抓取和人体运动模仿等下游应用。

Abstract: Dexterous grasping is usually conducted for specific tasks, leading to heterogeneous constraints such as specific approach directions, desired contact regions, specified wrist trajectories, and functional hand poses. Our previous work, GraspXL, achieves scalable grasping motion synthesis for diverse objects and hand morphologies, while lacking controllability for synthesis under such various task-driven constraints. In this paper, we propose ConGraspXL, which extends GraspXL with controllable constraint-conditioned grasp motion synthesis that accommodates diverse task-driven constraints and their combinations. We introduce a hierarchical constraint formulation, enable flexible constraint composition with a masked residual interface, and improve control precision with dynamic hand centers and feed-forward wrist guidance. Without losing the strong generalization capabilities of GraspXL, ConGraspXL enables precise and flexible controllability for various individual constraints and their combinations, providing a plug-and-play low-level grasp controller for downstream applications such as whole-body grasp completion, functional grasping, and human-motion imitation.

</details>


### [103] [ManiSkillFormer: Demonstration-Free Compositional Manipulation via Task-Conditioned Geometric Contracts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16331)
*Peiqi Yu,Mosam Dabhi,Shangtao Li,Bowei Li,Laszlo Jeni,Changliu Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出神经符号框架ManiSkillFormer，通过任务条件几何契约显式连接感知与动作，实现无需演示、可组合的机器人操作，在多种任务上超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统端到端视觉运动策略需要大量演示且难以泛化到新物体与新任务，缺乏可组合性与复用性，因此需要一种无需逐物体微调、无需额外机器人演示的操作框架。

Method: 引入任务条件几何契约，显式结构化感知与动作接口。每个操作技能声明所需语义几何基元（如关键点、表面法线）；由大语言模型代理生成契约及对应运动模板，契约引导感知模块从观测中定位任务相关3D基元，并实例化存入技能库的可复用运动模板。

Result: 在Galaxea R1-Lite双臂机器人上评估：8类30个实例的零样本抓放成功率88.24%，功能操作任务（拧、倒、按、折）平均75.00%，3个长时任务完成率50-80%，均高于基线及两种消融管线。

Conclusion: 该设计无需逐物体策略微调或额外演示，即可实现跨物体、跨任务的可组合与可复用操作。

Abstract: We present ManiSkillFormer, a neuro-symbolic framework for demonstration-free and compositional robotic manipulation. Instead of learning end-to-end visuomotor policies, ManiSkillFormer introduces task-conditioned geometric contracts that explicitly structure the interface between perception and action. Each manipulation skill declares the semantic geometric primitives required for execution, such as object keypoints and surface normals. Building on human-defined skill structures, LLM agents generate these contracts and corresponding motion templates for different objects and task contexts. These contracts guide the perception module to ground task-relevant 3D primitives from observations, which are then used to instantiate reusable motion templates stored in a skill library. We evaluate ManiSkillFormer on Galaxea R1-Lite dual-arm robot across three settings: zero-shot pick-and-place over 8 object categories with 30 different instances, functional manipulation tasks including unscrewing, pouring, pressing, and folding, and 3 long-horizon tasks. ManiSkillFormer achieves higher average success rates than the evaluated baselines and two ablated pipelines: 88.24% for demonstration-free pick-and-place, 75.00% average success on functional manipulation and 50--80% completion rates across the long-horizon tasks. These results show that our design enables composable and reusable manipulation across objects and tasks without per-object policy fine-tuning or additional robot demonstrations.

</details>


### [104] [Auto-HSI: Personalized human control of a robot swarm on demand by using LLMs for online automatic code generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16346)
*Alessandro Nazzari,Nathan Cerisara,Dorian Tonnis,Raina Zakir,Lorenzo Labarile,Weixu Zhu,Marco Dorigo,Mary Katherine Heinrich*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 Auto-HSI，一种按需生成个性化人-集群交互界面的方法，使未受训操作者仅用自然语言描述和手势演示即可自动生成控制机器人集群的个性化状态机代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人-集群交互界面通常需要专业技能或事先培训才能配置，未受训操作者难以按自身需求定制机器人集群的集体行为。

Method: Auto-HSI 接受自然语言描述与手势演示作为输入，自动生成个性化状态机代码，从而产生用于集中控制的交互界面；生成界面支持单手与双手手势，可遥操作机器人运动、队形形状及形状形变。论文对姿态跟踪与代码生成模块进行性能基准测试，并在基于物理的仿真器中由真实操作者集中控制50个机器人，在正常与噪声条件下开展实机（仿真）操作实验。

Result: 在实验中，操作者遥操作机器人完成进球、穿越需要形变的迷宫、以及分组同时攻入两个球门等任务；还演示了操作者在运行中实时更新其个性化 Auto-HSI 界面，并展示了真实机器人的实时运行。

Conclusion: Auto-HSI 证明了通过自然语言与手势自动生成个性化集群交互界面的可行性，能够支持未受训操作者在正常与噪声条件下完成多种复杂集群任务，并支持运行时界面更新与真实机器人部署。

Abstract: This paper presents Auto-HSI, a method for generating personalized human-swarm interaction (HSI) interfaces on demand. The objective is to enable untrained operators to use natural language descriptions and gesture demonstrations to explain how they want the robots to collectively behave in response to their gestures. Based on these inputs, the code should automatically be generated for personalized state machines that will control the robots as desired, in response to the desired gesture inputs. In the developed Auto-HSI prototype, the generated code produces a personalized interface for centralized control using one- and two-handed gestures, enabling a user to teleoperate the robots' motion, formation shape, and shape deformation. We test the gesture tracking and code generation components of Auto-HSI against performance benchmarks. We then test the full Auto-HSI prototype in ``live'' operation experiments, in which real human operators centrally control 50 simulated robots in a physics-based simulator, under nominal and noisy conditions. In these experiments, robots are teleoperated to: score a goal, traverse a maze that requires shape deformation, and score two simultaneous goals by splitting into two groups. We also demonstrate a real human operator making live updates to their personalized Auto-HSI interface during operation (in simulation). Finally, we demonstrate live operation of real robots.

</details>


### [105] [UDAV: Uncertainty-Driven Adaptive VLM Waypoint Planner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16368)
*Ghazal Farhani,Shabnam Shabani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: UDAV利用VLM随机轨迹预测的中位数与空间离散度构建不确定性信号，实现无人机引导地面车辆越野导航，平均位移误差较确定性规划降低25.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型可直接从航空影像生成越野导航路径，但其预测缺乏可靠性指示，难以判断何时可信、何时需重规划。论文旨在为VLM路径规划引入可用的不确定性度量，以选择性缓解大幅规划误差。

Method: 提出UDAV（不确定性驱动的自适应VLM航点规划器）：对同一查询采样多条随机轨迹预测，取其中位数（medoid）作为自一致的标称路径，并以这些轨迹的空间离散度估计预测不确定性；当内部航点的最大不确定性超过阈值时触发"重新审议"阶段，否则直接返回中位数路径。

Result: 在两次无人机飞行采集的400条留出轨迹查询上评估：随机中位数选择将平均位移误差（ADE）从确定性预测的147.4像素降至115.9像素；完整规划器平均ADE为110.4像素，相对确定性规划降低25.1%，且所有查询均生成有效轨迹；其第90与第95百分位误差在所有配置（含K=10共识基线）中最低，相对K=5中位数由225.3与326.0像素降至199.0与290.8像素。

Conclusion: 随机VLM预测既能提供更优的标称路径，也能提供可操作的不确定性信号，用于有选择地抑制大规划误差，从而提升越野导航的可靠性与稳健性。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) can generate routes directly from aerial imagery for off-road navigation, but their predictions provide no indication of reliability. We present UDAV, an Uncertainty-Driven Adaptive VLM Waypoint Planner for UAV-guided UGV navigation. UDAV draws multiple stochastic trajectory predictions, selects their medoid as a self-consistent nominal route, and estimates predictive uncertainty from their spatial dispersion. When the maximum uncertainty across interior waypoints exceeds a threshold, UDAV invokes a reconsideration stage; otherwise, it returns the medoid directly. We evaluate UDAV on 400 held-out trajectory queries from two UAV flights. Stochastic medoid selection reduces the mean average displacement error (ADE) from 147.4 pixels for a deterministic prediction to 115.9 pixels. The complete planner achieves a mean ADE of 110.4 pixels, a 25.1% reduction relative to deterministic planning, while producing valid trajectories for all queries. UDAV also yields the lowest 90th- and 95th-percentile errors among all evaluated configurations, including a higher-budget K=10 consensus baseline. Relative to the K=5 medoid, UDAV reduces these errors from 225.3 and 326.0 pixels to 199.0 and 290.8 pixels, respectively. These results demonstrate that stochastic VLM predictions provide both a stronger nominal route and an actionable uncertainty signal for selectively mitigating large planning errors.

</details>


### [106] [Geometry vs Structure: Graph-Based Diagnostics for LiDAR Point-Cloud Simulation Fidelity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16378)
*Ghazal Farhani,Taufiq Rahman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出基于图的框架，通过Louvain社区检测与图谱度量r_λ，评估仿真LiDAR点云相对真实扫描的结构保真度，发现几何与结构度量互补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字孪生可低成本补充自动驾驶与ADAS传感器验证，但3D LiDAR点云仿真保真度难以量化；传统几何指标（如Chamfer距离）忽略连通性、拓扑与对象级组织结构。

Method: 在真实与仿真点云上构建图，用Louvain社区检测识别空间连贯子图，并按质心邻近度匹配对应社区；对每对匹配社区计算有界图谱度量r_λ（基于Weyl不等式），并与密度感知Chamfer距离（CDC）几何基线对比；通过受控扰动实验验证性质。

Result: r_λ对刚性变换不变、对传感器噪声稳健且对结构形变敏感。在50对Velodyne VLP-32C与CARLA配对扫描、超1000个匹配社区（车辆、植被、树木、建筑墙面四类）上评估，显示几何与结构度量刻画仿真保真度的互补方面。

Conclusion: 图谱分析可作为数字孪生验证的附加诊断层，用于ADAS与自动驾驶应用中的仿真保真度评估。

Abstract: Digital twins provide a scalable and cost-effective complement to real-world testing for validating autonomous-driving and advanced driver-assistance system (ADAS) sensor pipelines. However, quantifying their fidelity remains challenging, particularly for 3D LiDAR point clouds, where conventional geometric metrics may overlook important structural discrepancies. We present a graph-based framework for evaluating the structural fidelity of simulated LiDAR point clouds against real-world scans. While scan-level metrics such as Chamfer distance capture point-wise geometric similarity, they do not explicitly represent connectivity, topology, or object-level organization. Our framework constructs graphs from real and simulated point clouds, applies Louvain community detection to identify spatially coherent subgraphs, and matches corresponding communities using centroid proximity. For each matched pair, we compute $r_λ$, a bounded graph-spectral metric motivated by Weyl's inequality, and compare it with density-aware Chamfer distance (CDC) as a geometric baseline. Controlled perturbation experiments demonstrate that $r_λ$ is invariant to rigid transformations and robust to sensor noise while remaining sensitive to structural deformation. We evaluate the framework on 50 paired real and simulated LiDAR scans acquired using a Velodyne VLP-32C sensor and CARLA, respectively. The dataset contains more than 1,000 matched communities across four representative classes: vehicles, vegetation, trees, and building walls. The results show that geometric and structural measures capture complementary aspects of simulation fidelity, supporting graph-spectral analysis as an additional diagnostic layer for validating digital twins in ADAS and autonomous-driving applications.

</details>


### [107] [Collision-Aware Humanoid Whole-Body Control under Imperfect Tracking Targets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16405)
*Mohitvishnu S. Gadde,Ashish Malik,Pranay Dugar,Aayam Kumar Shrestha,Alan Fern*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RECAL——一种机器人-环境交叉注意力层，包裹现成的全身控制器（WBC），在跟踪目标与避碰之间权衡，实现几何感知的运动控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数人形机器人WBC在跟踪目标时对场景几何"视而不见"，当目标运动因感知、规划或遥操作误差而几何不安全时，容易导致碰撞。

Method: RECAL将机器人、所持物体和环境表示为点云，通过机器人/对象点与环境点之间的交叉注意力生成几何感知的控制特征，从而包装盲WBC，支持浮动基座与末端执行器命令的避碰跟踪（含所持物体的避碰）。

Result: 在仿真中，相对于其他几何感知WBC架构，RECAL在冻结臂与自适应臂行走、物体搬运、站立操作等场景下，在提升避碰能力的同时保持了目标跟踪性能；并在真实Digit V3人形机器人上进行了演示。

Conclusion: RECAL可作为通用模块为现成WBC赋予场景几何感知能力，在保持跟踪性能的前提下有效提升避碰安全性，并具备真机可行性。

Abstract: Humanoid robots often execute motion commands through whole-body controllers (WBCs) that track targets while maintaining balance and stability.
